{"antonyms":[{"sense":"antonym(s) of “order theory”","word":"ideal"}],"forms":[{"form":"filters","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["A device which separates a suspended, dissolved, or particulate matter from a fluid, solution, or other substance; any device that separates one substance from another."]},{"glosses":["Electronics or software that separates unwanted signals (for example noise) from wanted signals or that attenuates selected frequencies."]},{"examples":[{"text":"He runs an email filter to catch the junk mail.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Any item, mechanism, device, or procedure that acts to separate or isolate."]},{"examples":[{"text":"He's got no filter, and he's always offending people as a result.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Self-restraint in speech."],"tags":["figuratively"]},{"examples":[{"text":"The collection of cofinite subsets of ℝ is a filter under inclusion: it includes the intersection of every pair of its members, and includes every superset of every cofinite set.","type":"example"},{"text":"If (1) the universal set (here, the set of natural numbers) were called a \"large\" set, (2) the superset of any \"large\" set were also a \"large\" set, and (3) the intersection of a pair of \"large\" sets were also a \"large\" set, then the set of all \"large\" sets would form a filter.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A non-empty upper set (of a partially ordered set) which is closed under binary infima (a.k.a. meets)."],"topics":["mathematics","order-theory","sciences"]},{"glosses":["A translucent object placed in the light path of a camera to remove certain wavelengths (colors), or a computer program that simulates such an effect."],"topics":["arts","hobbies","lifestyle","photography"]},{"glosses":["An appearance-altering digital image effect."],"qualifier":"social media"}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ˈfɪltə/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/ˈfɪltɚ/","tags":["General-American"]}],"upstream_line":190624,"upstream_offset":916452675,"upstream_record_sha256":"eb6f5c1478bb3f863f476b0a9e4cc601518de74cee339f18feef41f4faf57cd6","word":"filter"}
{"forms":[{"form":"filters","tags":["present","singular","third-person"]},{"form":"filtering","tags":["participle","present"]},{"form":"filtered","tags":["participle","past"]},{"form":"filtered","tags":["past"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"verb","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"This strainer should filter out the large particles.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To sort, sift, or isolate."],"tags":["transitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"The leaves of the trees filtered the light.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To diffuse; to cause to be less concentrated or focused."],"tags":["transitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"The water filtered through the rock and soil.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To pass through a filter or to act as though passing through a filter."],"tags":["intransitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"The crowd filtered into the theater.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To move slowly or gradually; to come or go a few at a time."],"tags":["intransitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"I can skip past all the traffic on my bike by filtering.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To ride a motorcycle between lanes on a road"],"tags":["intransitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Most people get filtered by that episode.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To be discouraged where a connoisseur or hardcore fan would not."],"tags":["derogatory","intransitive","slang"],"topics":["lifestyle"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ˈfɪltə/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/ˈfɪltɚ/","tags":["General-American"]}],"synonyms":[{"sense":"to sort, sift, or isolate","word":"to filter out"}],"upstream_line":190625,"upstream_offset":916494111,"upstream_record_sha256":"bbd00c600732aa6073ca8cfda5327e573ca3aefa40962bdf20f8a730fd82919e","word":"filter"}
